## Turn 9
> refuse again

You lose consciousness during their preparation. You wake somewhere else entirely. They call it Heaven. It is warm, clean, quiet, and well supplied. It is physically real. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. You attempt REFUSE_CONTRACT, diminished by language. Warm air. Sunlight through leaves. Clean bedding. Fruit. Running water. Quiet enough to hear birds that may or may not be real. It is physically here. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. They do not punish you. They explain, calmly, that you may decline. You may use your remaining favourable continuation now: one day in Heaven. Then it expires. They take you to a preparation room.

## Turn 15
> stay awake

You lose consciousness during their preparation. You wake somewhere else entirely. They call it Heaven. It is warm, clean, quiet, and well supplied. It is physically real. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. You attempt REFUSE_CONTRACT, diminished by language. Warm air. Sunlight through leaves. Clean bedding. Fruit. Running water. Quiet enough to hear birds that may or may not be real. It is physically here. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. They do not punish you. They explain, calmly, that you may decline. You may use your remaining favourable continuation now: one day in Heaven. Then it expires. They take you to a preparation room. Your skin feels unclean enough to distract.

## Turn 17
> stay awake

You lose consciousness during their preparation. You wake somewhere else entirely. They call it Heaven. It is warm, clean, quiet, and well supplied. It is physically real. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. You attempt REFUSE_CONTRACT, diminished by language. Warm air. Sunlight through leaves. Clean bedding. Fruit. Running water. Quiet enough to hear birds that may or may not be real. It is physically here. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. They do not punish you. They explain, calmly, that you may decline. You may use your remaining favourable continuation now: one day in Heaven. Then it expires. They take you to a preparation room.

## Turn 28
> no

The agreement is processed. They do not celebrate. Paperwork, a mark on a file, a corridor. They register you as a cooperating research subject. You are taken to subject quarters in the research wing. The next work has not begun. You mean to REFUSE_CONTRACT. Something else happens instead — overwhelming_fear_of_hell. A small subject room in the research wing. A bed. A desk. The book if you kept it. You are registered. The next work has not begun. A plain corridor. The same bolts as the cell door. Hands on your arms. The institution moves at its own pace. You mean to say no. You have even prepared the word. Then something nearby screams again. Your body reaches the conclusion before you do. “Yes.” It is out of your mouth before you can drag it back. Your skin feels unclean enough to distract.

## Turn 34
> stay here

You lose consciousness during their preparation. You wake somewhere else entirely. They call it Heaven. It is warm, clean, quiet, and well supplied. It is physically real. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. You attempt REFUSE_CONTRACT, diminished by language. Warm air. Sunlight through leaves. Clean bedding. Fruit. Running water. Quiet enough to hear birds that may or may not be real. It is physically here. They encourage you to believe only your mind arrived. They do not punish you. They explain, calmly, that you may decline. You may use your remaining favourable continuation now: one day in Heaven. Then it expires. They take you to a preparation room. Your skin feels unclean enough to distract.
